 {"content": "# Workflow: Officiating\n\nEnd-to-end lifecycle for managing referees — from certification registration\nthrough match assignment, confirmation, completion, and shortage alerting.\n\n## Overview\n\nThe **officiating** domain tracks referees (`federation.referee`), their\ncertification records (`federation.referee.certification`), and per-match role\nassignments (`federation.match.referee`). When a referee is assigned to a\nmatch, they receive an automated notification. If the assignment is not\nconfirmed before the deadline, the system flags it as overdue and optionally\nsends an alert to the referee coordinator.\n\nThis is a specialist coordinator workflow, not the primary day-to-day starting\nsurface for club representatives. Club operators should usually encounter\nofficiating readiness through the phase-specific workspaces and alerts rather\nthan begin from officiating lists.\n\n## Modules Involved\n\n| Module | Role |\n|--------|------|\n| `sports_federation_officiating` | Referee registry, certifications, match assignments |\n| `sports_federation_tournament` | Match records |\n| `sports_federation_notifications` | Referee assignment and shortage alert dispatching |\n\n## Models\n\n| Model | Purpose |\n|-------|---------|\n| `federation.referee` | Master referee record with contact info and certification level |\n| `federation.referee.certification` | Dated certification for a specific officiating level |\n| `federation.match.referee` | Assignment of one referee to one match in a specific role |\n\n## Step-by-Step Flow\n\n### 1. Referee Registration\n\n**Actor**: Federation administrator\n**Module**: `sports_federation_officiating`\n\n1. Navigate to **Federation → Officiating → Referees** and click **New**.\n2. Enter name, contact details (email, phone, mobile).\n3. Set `certification_level` to the highest level held:\n   `local` | `regional` | `national` | `international`.\n4. Save. The referee is immediately available for match assignments.\n\n### 2. Certification Records\n\n**Actor**: Federation administrator\n**Module**: `sports_federation_officiating`\n\n1. On the referee form, open the **Certifications** tab.\n2. Add one `federation.referee.certification` line per certification granted:\n   - Certification number (unique per referee / level / issue date)\n   - Level (matches the levels on the referee itself)\n   - Issue date and optional expiry date\n   - Issuing body\n3. Active certifications (`active = True`) are considered valid.\n4. An expired certification (`expiry_date` in the past) should be archived\n   (`active = False`) by an admin; the system does not auto-archive.\n\nCertification uniqueness: `(referee_id, level, issue_date)` — one certification\nper level per issuance date.\n\n### 3. Match Assignment\n\n**Actor**: Federation referee coordinator\n**Module**: `sports_federation_officiating`\n\n1. Open the match record in **Federation → Tournaments → Matches**.\n2. In the **Referees** tab (or from the Referees list), click **Add a line**.\n3. Select the referee and assign a **role**:\n   - `head` — Head Referee\n   - `assistant_1` / `assistant_2` — Assistant Referees\n   - `fourth` — Fourth Official\n   - `table` — Table Official\n4. Save. The assignment starts in state **`draft`** (\"Assigned\").\n5. On creation, the notification dispatcher fires `send_referee_assigned` if\n   `federation.notification.dispatcher` is available, sending an assignment\n   notice to the referee.\n\nAssignment uniqueness: `(match_id, referee_id, role)` — one role per referee per match.\n\n### 4. Readiness Check\n\n**Actor**: System (computed field)\n**Module**: `sports_federation_officiating`\n\nAfter assignment, the system computes:\n- `confirmation_deadline` — 48 hours before the scheduled match kickoff.\n- `is_confirmation_overdue` — True if past deadline and still in `draft`.\n- `assignment_ready` — True when the assignment is `confirmed`, the referee\n  is active, and holds a valid certification for the match's required level.\n- `readiness_feedback` — human-readable summary of any blocking reasons.\n\nThese fields re-compute whenever match scheduling, referee certification, or\nassignment state changes.\n\n### 5. Referee Confirmation\n\n**Actor**: Referee or federation administrator\n**Module**: `sports_federation_officiating`\n\n1. Referee or administrator sets the assignment state: **`draft` → `confirmed`**.\n2. `confirmed_on` timestamp is recorded.\n3. `assignment_ready` becomes True (assuming valid certification and active referee).\n\n### 6. Overdue / Shortage Detection\n\n**Actor**: System (notification scan) or administrator\n**Module**: `sports_federation_officiating` + `sports_federation_notifications`\n\n1. Assignments with `is_confirmation_overdue = True` are surfaced as a warning\n   in the coordinator's dashboard and in the readiness feedback.\n2. If the `sports_federation_notifications` module is installed, a scheduled\n   notification scan can detect unconfirmed assignments and send alerts.\n3. The coordinator resolves shortages by:\n   - Following up with the originally assigned referee, OR\n   - Cancelling the assignment (`draft` → `cancelled`) and assigning a replacement.\n\n### 7. Match Completion\n\n**Actor**: System or federation administrator (after match result entry)\n**Module**: `sports_federation_officiating`\n\n1. When a match is completed (state → `done`), assignments are marked **`done`**.\n2. `completed_on` timestamp is recorded.\n3. `done` assignments contribute to `assignment_count` on the referee record for\n   performance and reporting purposes.\n\n### 8. Cancellation\n\n**Actor**: Federation coordinator\n**Module**: `sports_federation_officiating`\n\n1. Any assignment in `draft` or `confirmed` can be cancelled by setting state → **`cancelled`**.\n2. `cancelled_on` timestamp is recorded.\n3. Cancelled assignments remain in the record for audit purposes but are excluded\n   from the assignment count and readiness checks.\n\n## State Diagram\n\n```\nAssignment: draft (Assigned) → confirmed → done\n                             ↘ cancelled\n            draft → cancelled\n```\n\n## Certification States\n\n```\nCertification: active=True  (valid, current)\n               active=False (archived/expired — set manually by administrator)\n```\n\n## Notification Triggers\n\n| Event | Notification |\n|-------|-------------|\n| Assignment created | `send_referee_assigned` via notification dispatcher |\n| Assignment overdue | Alert to referee coordinator (notification scan) |\n\n## Key Decision Points\n\n| Question | Outcome |\n|----------|---------|\n| No active certification at required level? | `assignment_ready = False`; readiness feedback lists reason |\n| Confirmation deadline passed, still `draft`? | `is_confirmation_overdue = True`; flagged in dashboard |\n| Another assignment overlaps the same match window? | `assignment_ready = False`; confirmation is blocked until the clash is resolved |\n| Match cancelled? | Coordinator cancels all assignments; `cancelled_on` recorded |\n| Referee deactivated (`active = False`)? | All future assignments blocked; `assignment_ready = False` |\n\n## Referee Availability & Self-Service (Specialist / Planned)\n\n> **Note**: The availability model is a planned feature. The fields and steps\n> below describe the intended design; the `federation.referee.availability`\n> model may not yet be present in all installations. Check the module README\n> for the current implementation status.\n\n### Marking Availability Windows\n\n**Actor**: Referee (portal) or federation coordinator (back office)\n**Module**: `sports_federation_officiating` + `sports_federation_portal`\n\n1. Referee logs into the federation portal.\n2. Navigates to **My Availability** in the officiating section.\n3. Creates `federation.referee.availability` records specifying:\n   - **Start** and **End** datetime of the available window\n   - Optional notes (e.g. \"available only from 14:00\")\n4. Saves. Availability records are immediately visible to the coordinator.\n\nAlternatively, the coordinator creates availability records on behalf of the\nreferee from the back office (**Federation → Officiating → Referees → Availability tab**).\n\n### Assignment Engine Conflict Check\n\n**Actor**: System (at assignment creation time)\n**Module**: `sports_federation_officiating`\n\nWhen the coordinator assigns a referee to a match:\n\n1. The system checks whether the match's scheduled kickoff falls within any\n   `federation.referee.availability` window for that referee.\n2. If the match falls **outside** all recorded availability windows, a warning\n   is shown on the assignment form: *\"No availability record covers this match\n   slot.\"*\n3. The coordinator can override the warning and save the assignment (warnings\n   do not block creation).\n4. If no availability records exist for a referee, no conflict check is\n   performed and no warning is raised.\n5. If the referee is already assigned to another match that overlaps the same\n   assignment window, the draft assignment can still be recorded for follow-up,\n   but `assignment_ready` becomes `False` and confirmation is blocked until the\n   clash is resolved.\n6. The scheduling workflow reuses the same officiating checks so double-booking\n   blocks schedule readiness while uncovered availability remains visible as a\n   warning. The officiating addon remains the owner of these checks.\n\n### Portal Accept / Decline Flow\n\n**Actor**: Referee (portal)\n**Module**: `sports_federation_officiating` + `sports_federation_portal`\n\nAfter an assignment is created in `draft` state and the referee receives the\nassignment notification:\n\n1. Referee opens the notification or navigates to **My Assignments** in the portal.\n   - The portal assignments list highlights one explicit **Next step** action\n     (nearest pending response) and shows pending/overdue counters so officials\n     can prioritize urgent confirmations first.\n2. Views the match date, venue, and role.\n3. Clicks **Confirm** or **Decline**:\n   - **Confirm**: assignment state `draft → confirmed`.\n   - **Decline**: assignment state `draft → cancelled`. The coordinator is\n     notified to assign a replacement.\n\nBoth actions are available until the `confirmation_deadline` (48 hours before\nkickoff) passes.\n\n### Confirmation Deadline and Overdue Escalation\n\n**Actor**: System + federation coordinator\n**Module**: `sports_federation_officiating` + `sports_federation_notifications`\n\nIf the referee does not confirm or decline before the `confirmation_deadline`:\n\n1. `is_confirmation_overdue` becomes `True` on the assignment.\n2. The assignment is surfaced in the coordinator's overdue-assignment dashboard\n   filter (**Federation → Officiating → Assignments → Overdue**).\n3. If `sports_federation_notifications` is installed, the scheduled notification\n   scan sends an overdue alert to the coordinator.\n4. The coordinator:\n   - Contacts the referee directly, OR\n   - Cancels the assignment and assigns a replacement.\n\nThere is no auto-cancel on deadline expiry. The coordinator makes the final\ndecision on shortage resolution.\n\n## Related Workflows\n\n- [Match Day Operations](WORKFLOW_MATCH_DAY_OPERATIONS.md) — referee assignment within the broader match-day sequence\n- [Tournament Lifecycle](WORKFLOW_TOURNAMENT_LIFECYCLE.md) — match scheduling that triggers officiating needs\n", "content_length": 11160, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "_workflows/WORKFLOW_OFFICIATING.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "a7b9fe91bbca347ac81e29e7707215af91c47e3505ef959e298e905d7494f6e3", "size_bytes": 11228}
 {"content": "# Workflow: Player License Lifecycle\n\nEnd-to-end management of a player's federation license — from creation through\nseasonal activation, expiry, suspension checks, and reinstatement.\n\n## Overview\n\nEvery player who competes in a federation tournament must hold a valid\n**player license** (`federation.player.license`) scoped to the current season.\nA license certifies the player's eligibility, links them to their club, and\nrecords their category (Senior, Youth, Junior, Cadet). The license state machine\ndrives eligibility checks used by the roster and rules engines.\n\n## Modules Involved\n\n| Module | Role |\n|--------|------|\n| `sports_federation_people` | Player records and license model |\n| `sports_federation_base` | Season and club master data |\n| `sports_federation_rules` | Eligibility service (`federation.eligibility.service`) |\n| `sports_federation_rosters` | Roster-line license validation (`assignment_ready`) |\n| `sports_federation_officiating` | Suspension check integration |\n| `sports_federation_discipline` | Suspension sanctions that block eligibility |\n| `sports_federation_finance_bridge` | License-fee finance events |\n| `sports_federation_notifications` | Expiry and reinstatement notifications |\n\n## Models\n\n| Model | Key Fields |\n|-------|-----------|\n| `federation.player.license` | `name` (license number), `player_id`, `season_id`, `club_id`, `state`, `category`, `issue_date`, `expiry_date` |\n| `federation.player` | `state` (`active` / `inactive` / `suspended`), `is_eligible` (computed) |\n\n## License States\n\n```\ndraft → active → expired\n      ↘ cancelled\nactive → cancelled\n```\n\n| State | Meaning |\n|-------|---------|\n| `draft` | License created but not yet active — player is not eligible |\n| `active` | License is valid — player is eligible for roster inclusion |\n| `expired` | Expiry date passed and license was not renewed — player is not eligible |\n| `cancelled` | License administratively voided — player is not eligible |\n\n## Step-by-Step Flow\n\n### 1. License Creation\n\n**Actor**: Federation administrator or club representative (portal)\n**Module**: `sports_federation_people`\n\n1. Navigate to **Federation → People → Player Licenses** and click **New**, or\n   open the player record and click **Create License** from the **Licenses** tab.\n2. Fill in:\n   - **License Number** — unique identifier (e.g. `LIC-2026-00042`)\n   - **Player** — select the player\n   - **Season** — must be an open season\n   - **Club** — the club the player is registered under this season\n   - **Category** — `Senior`, `Youth`, `Junior`, or `Cadet`\n   - **Issue Date** — defaults to today\n   - **Expiry Date** — set to the season end date or a specific date\n3. Save. The license starts in **`draft`** state.\n\n**Uniqueness constraint**: one license per player per season.\nAttempting a second license for the same player/season raises a validation error.\n\n### 2. Season Activation\n\n**Actor**: Federation administrator\n**Module**: `sports_federation_people`\n\n1. After verifying any required compliance documents or fee payments, click\n   **Activate** on the license form.\n2. License state transitions: **`draft` → `active`**.\n3. The player's computed `is_eligible` field becomes `True`.\n4. The player is now available for roster assignment under that season.\n\n**Finance event**: if `sports_federation_finance_bridge` is installed, a license\nfee event (`category = registration`) is created automatically on activation.\n\n### 3. Eligibility Check Integration\n\n**Actor**: System (called by roster and rules modules)\n**Module**: `sports_federation_rules`\n\nThe `federation.eligibility.service` is the authoritative eligibility oracle:\n\n1. On roster-line creation or tournament participant validation, the service\n   calls `_is_player_eligible(player, season)`.\n2. The service checks:\n   - `federation.player.license` with `state = active` and matching `season_id`.\n   - `federation.player.state` not equal to `suspended`.\n   - No active suspension sanction from `sports_federation_discipline`.\n3. If any check fails, the roster line is marked `assignment_ready = False` with\n   a human-readable `readiness_feedback`.\n\n### 4. Expiry Rules\n\n**Actor**: System\n**Module**: `sports_federation_people`\n\n- An active license whose `expiry_date` has passed is shown with a visual\n  warning on the player and license forms.\n- The system does **not** auto-transition `active → expired`. An administrator\n  must manually click **Mark Expired** or run the `_cron_expire_licenses`\n  scheduled action (if configured).\n- Expired licenses are excluded from eligibility checks.\n\n**Season close**: when a season is closed, it is good practice to run\n`_cron_expire_licenses` immediately to clean up all active licenses tied to that\nseason.\n\n### 5. Suspension Check Integration\n\n**Actor**: System\n**Module**: `sports_federation_discipline`\n\n1. When a disciplinary sanction with `sanction_type = suspension` and\n   `state = active` exists for a player, the eligibility service flags that\n   player as ineligible regardless of license state.\n2. The player's `state` is set to `suspended` by the discipline module's\n   enforcement action.\n3. All roster lines for that player in upcoming matches are flagged\n   `assignment_ready = False`.\n\n### 6. Reinstatement Path\n\n**Actor**: Federation administrator\n**Module**: `sports_federation_people` + `sports_federation_discipline`\n\nA player can be reinstated after a suspension or licence cancellation:\n\n1. **Suspension ended**: the discipline module expires the suspension sanction\n   and calls `action_reactivate` on the player, transitioning\n   `suspended → active`.\n2. **Cancelled licence renewed**: create a new draft licence for the player,\n   activate it. The old cancelled licence remains in history.\n3. **Expired licence renewed for the same season**: cancel the expired licence,\n   create a new draft licence, and activate it. (The unique constraint is per\n   player/season so the old expired record must be cancelled first.)\n4. On reinstatement, the player's `is_eligible` recomputes automatically.\n\n**Notification**: if `sports_federation_notifications` is installed, the\nreinstatement triggers a notification to the club contact on record.\n\n## Eligibility Summary\n\nA player is eligible to compete when **all** of the following are true:\n\n- `federation.player.state = active`\n- At least one `federation.player.license` with `state = active` and\n  `season_id` matching the competition season\n- No active suspension sanction\n\n## State Diagram\n\n```\nLicense: draft → active → expired (manual or cron)\n               ↘ cancelled\n         active → cancelled\n\nPlayer:  active → suspended → active (reinstatement)\n         active → inactive\n```\n", "content_length": 6675, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "_workflows/WORKFLOW_PLAYER_LICENSE.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "6cb3d2a62a95831f6a71074cea52a58211ced4121463d102bcc3327e59ba64f8", "size_bytes": 6731}
 {"content": "# Workflow: Public Publication\n\nPublishing tournaments, standings, schedules, and editorial coverage to the\npublic website.\n\n## Overview\n\nThe federation website exposes published competition coverage without requiring\nlogin. Publication is slug-first and tournament-first: operators publish a\ntournament, control which result and standing surfaces are visible, and can add\neditorial content around seasons, tournaments, or teams without editing website\ntemplates.\n\nReadiness handoff for publication should reuse the canonical chain from\nregistration through approved standings. Publication surfaces should add only\nthe final publication checklist, not duplicate every upstream prerequisite\nexplanation.\n\n## Modules Involved\n\n| Module | Role |\n|--------|------|\n| `sports_federation_public_site` | Publication fields, editorial items, website controllers, templates |\n| `sports_federation_tournament` | Tournament, match, and participant data |\n| `sports_federation_standings` | Standings data |\n| `sports_federation_result_control` | Approved results only |\n| `sports_federation_notifications` | Tournament-publication emails |\n| `sports_federation_venues` | Venue data on schedule pages |\n| `website` | Odoo website framework |\n\n## Step-by-Step Flow\n\n### 1. Tournament Publication Setup\n\n**Actor**: Federation administrator\n**Module**: `sports_federation_public_site`\n\nBefore publishing, ensure the tournament has:\n\n1. a completed setup with matches and public-facing text\n2. approved results if the results page should be visible\n3. computed standings if the standings page should be visible\n\nThen configure the public-site fields on the tournament:\n\n- `website_published`\n- `public_slug`\n- `public_description`\n- `public_featured`\n- `public_editorial_summary`\n- `public_pinned_announcement`\n- `show_public_results`\n- `show_public_standings`\n\nThe first transition from unpublished to published sends the tournament\npublication notification to participating club and team contacts.\n\nThe tournament Website tab now also surfaces the next unmet prerequisite\ndirectly on the form: whether the tournament itself is still unpublished,\nwhether standings do not exist yet, or whether standings exist but are not yet\npublished or visible.\n\n### 2. Standings And Results Visibility\n\n**Actor**: Federation administrator\n**Modules**: `sports_federation_standings`, `sports_federation_result_control`\n\n1. Publish the standings record itself with `website_published = True`.\n2. Optionally set `public_title` for the public standings page.\n3. Remember the public results page only shows approved results.\n4. Use the standings form and the tournament Website tab together for the final\n  handoff: standings pages explain whether approved results are still pending,\n  while the tournament Website tab explains whether the public blocker is the\n  standings record or the tournament visibility toggles.\n4. Public participant lists exclude withdrawn tournament participants.\n\n### 3. Editorial Publication Workflow\n\n**Actor**: Federation administrator or communications staff\n**Module**: `sports_federation_public_site`\n\nEditorial items can be anchored to a season, tournament, or team and use the\nstate machine below:\n\n`draft` → `scheduled` → `published` → `archived`\n\nOperator actions:\n\n1. **Schedule** — allowed only from `draft`, and only when `publish_start` is set.\n2. **Publish Now** — allowed from `draft` or `scheduled`.\n3. **Archive** — allowed from `scheduled` or `published`.\n4. **Reset to Draft** — allowed from `scheduled` or `archived`.\n\nStatusbar behavior:\n\n- The form statusbar shows the active publication pipeline:\n  `draft`, `scheduled`, `published`.\n- `archived` is still a real record state, but operators reach it and leave it\n  through explicit actions and search filters rather than as a visible statusbar\n  stop.\n\nVisibility guards:\n\n- Editorial items must be `active`.\n- `draft` and `archived` items are never public.\n- `scheduled` and `published` items are only public inside the\n  `publish_start` / `publish_end` window.\n\n### 4. Public Pages Go Live\n\n**Actor**: System (automatic)\n**Module**: `sports_federation_public_site`\n\nCanonical public surfaces include:\n\n| URL | Content |\n|-----|---------|\n| `/competitions` | Main tournament hub with featured, live, recent, and archived sections |\n| `/competitions/<edition-slug>` | Canonical competition edition overview page |\n| `/competitions/<edition-slug>/teams` | Published participant list excluding withdrawn entries |\n| `/competitions/<edition-slug>/standings` | Public standings page when enabled |\n| `/competitions/<edition-slug>/results` | Approved public results when enabled |\n| `/competitions/<edition-slug>/schedule` | Upcoming fixtures |\n| `/competitions/<edition-slug>/bracket` | Public bracket view when bracket data exists |\n| `/competitions/<edition-slug>/divisions/<division-slug>/schedule.ics` | Tournament schedule calendar export |\n| `/api/v1/competitions/<edition-slug>` | Stable v1 JSON feed |\n| `/seasons/<slug>` | Season landing page with editorial and tournament aggregation |\n| `/teams/<slug>` | Public team profile page |\n| `/teams/<slug>/schedule` | Team-centric grouped upcoming schedule |\n| `/teams/<slug>/results` | Team-centric recent approved results |\n| `/teams/<slug>/schedule.ics` | Team schedule calendar export |\n| `/api/v1/teams/<slug>/feed` | Stable v1 team follow feed |\n\nOlder `/competitions` and numeric routes remain as compatibility paths, but the\nauthoritative public URLs are the slug-based `/competitions/...` routes.\nCompatibility routes only redirect while the tournament still matches the\nrelevant publication guard; unpublished tournaments fail closed instead of\nresolving through direct slug or numeric paths. Season detail routes apply the\nsame fail-closed rule for both `/seasons/<slug>` and numeric `/season/<id>`\ncompatibility paths, and team profile/follow routes only resolve teams that\nstill have a published competition footprint.\n\n### 5. Ongoing Updates\n\n**Actor**: Federation administrator\n\nAs the tournament progresses:\n\n1. Approved results automatically feed the public result surfaces.\n2. Recomputed standings refresh public tables.\n3. Featured, live, recent, and archived hub sections update from current data.\n4. Publication emails are only sent on the publish transition itself, not on\n   every later content change.\n\n### 6. Archive And Removal\n\n**Actor**: Federation administrator\n\n1. Published tournaments in `closed` or `cancelled` move into the public archive\n   sections while `website_published` remains enabled.\n2. Setting `website_published = False` removes public access immediately.\n3. Editorial items can be archived without unpublishing the tournament itself.\n\n## Publication Checklist\n\n| Item | Required | Module |\n|------|----------|--------|\n| Tournament exists and is website-published | Yes | `public_site` |\n| `public_slug` set and unique | Yes | `public_site` |\n| Approved results available | Yes, for results page | `result_control` |\n| Tournament results visibility enabled | Optional | `public_site` |\n| Standings record website-published | Yes, for standings page | `standings` |\n| Tournament standings visibility enabled | Optional | `public_site` |\n| Editorial item linked to season/competition-division/team | Yes, for editorial content | `public_site` |\n| Editorial item publish window valid | Yes, when scheduling | `public_site` |\n\n## Exception and Recovery Model\n\n- Governance Override is the canonical exception engine when an operator needs\n  an approved exception path.\n- If results are disputed or corrected after publication, treat public\n  visibility as a recovery loop: rollback visibility if required, resolve the\n  dispute and approval chain, then republish standings/competition surfaces from\n  the owning records.\n\n## Access Control\n\n| Access | Level |\n|--------|-------|\n| Public pages | No authentication required |\n| Publication settings and editorial actions | Federation administrator or owning backend staff |\n| Data changes | Backend users only |\n\nPublic pages are read-only snapshots. Controllers resolve tournament, season,\nand team records through publication- and visibility-scoped queries before\nredirects, rendering, or feed and registration reads.\n\nRegistration validation feedback returns to the same division-specific form,\nincluding when the URL already carries a `division_id` selector. Capacity,\neligibility, and CSRF validation errors therefore remain actionable instead of\nredirecting to a malformed URL.\n\n## Related Workflows\n\n- [Tournament Lifecycle](WORKFLOW_TOURNAMENT_LIFECYCLE.md) — tournament setup and closure\n- [Result Pipeline](WORKFLOW_RESULT_PIPELINE.md) — result approval before publication\n- [Season Registration](WORKFLOW_SEASON_REGISTRATION.md) — published seasons and club context\n", "content_length": 8786, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "_workflows/WORKFLOW_PUBLIC_PUBLICATION.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "b1a92334ad96309aa5d855506f769f15e6ffef94d81abf8a365fce3de89f43ac", "size_bytes": 8806}

-{"content": "# Match-Day Operations\n\nThis addon executes the current immutable schedule publication. It never edits\napproved publication evidence.\n\n## Operator workflow\n\n- **Match-Day Control** shows readiness and the exact live publication.\n- **Open Match Day** validates the publication digest and creates an immutable\n  execution session.\n- Court status and incidents are changed through command-backed wizards.\n- Operational schedule changes support move, delay, postpone and cancel.\n- Every deviation requires a reason and records the session, publication, actor,\n  old slot and new slot.\n- The immutable `published_slot_id` is preserved. Live reality is represented by\n  `operational_slot_id` and `operational_status`.\n- Normal close requires all matches finished or cancelled and all incidents\n  resolved. Forced close requires a reason.\n\n## Validation\n\n```bash\npython ci/check_schedule_handoff_contract.py\npython ci/check_publication_integrity_contract.py\nbash ci/run_tests.sh --module sports_federation_schedule_approval\nbash ci/run_tests.sh --module sports_federation_matchday\n```\n\n## Restarting unpublished planning\n\nMatch-day managers can use **Restart from Scratch** before any schedule review or\npublication evidence exists. The action requires a reason and explicit destructive\nconfirmation, removes mutable schedules and the old match day, then creates and\nopens a clean replacement with the same edition, date, venue, and slot defaults.\nPublished, reviewed, open, and closed match days remain protected and must use the\nnormal revision or operational correction workflows.\n\n", "content_length": 1578, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "sports_federation_matchday/README.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "f166f0e2ae00212fa453367d64b44d77a3f88b5fbb11c51913f67b31f3f1fbcd", "size_bytes": 1578}
+{"content": "# Match-Day Operations\n\nThis addon executes the current immutable schedule publication. It never edits\napproved publication evidence.\n\n## Operator workflow\n\n- **Match-Day Control** shows readiness and the exact live publication.\n- **Open Match Day** validates the publication digest and creates an immutable\n  execution session.\n- Court status and incidents are changed through command-backed wizards.\n- Operational schedule changes support move, delay, postpone and cancel.\n- Every deviation requires a reason and records the session, publication, actor,\n  old slot and new slot.\n- The immutable `published_slot_id` is preserved. Live reality is represented by\n  `operational_slot_id` and `operational_status`.\n- Normal close requires all matches finished or cancelled and all incidents\n  resolved. Forced close requires a reason.\n\n## Validation\n\n```bash\npython ci/check_schedule_handoff_contract.py\npython ci/check_publication_integrity_contract.py\nbash ci/run_tests.sh --module sports_federation_schedule_approval\nbash ci/run_tests.sh --module sports_federation_matchday\n```\n\n## Restarting unpublished planning\n\nMatch-day managers can use **Restart from Scratch** before any schedule review or\npublication evidence exists. The action requires a reason and explicit destructive\nconfirmation, removes mutable schedules and the old match day, then creates and\nopens a clean replacement with the same edition, date, venue, and slot defaults.\nDirect deletion of a draft match day also removes its draft or change-requested\nworking schedules first. Published, reviewed, open, and closed match days remain\nprotected and must use the normal revision or operational correction workflows.\n\n## Deleting closed test data\n\nFederation managers and match-day managers can use **Delete Match Day** on a\nclosed match day. The confirmation wizard shows the number of schedules,\npublications, execution sessions, deviations, and matches affected. Confirming\nthe deletion permanently removes the match-day planning and execution evidence;\nlinked match records remain in the competition but are detached from the deleted\npublication and calendar slots. The action requires a reason and displays a\nfinal confirmation popup before deletion.\n\n", "content_length": 2222, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "modified", "path": "sports_federation_matchday/README.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "58a7c9ce66c0053b6ffb9c8f4f6aea58fdef8df040d9e470f0e479bfaf237c31", "size_bytes": 2222}

_name = \"federation.matchday.commands\"\n    _description = \"Match-Day Operations Commands\"\n\n    def _resolve(self, matchday_id):\n        matchday = self.env[\"federation.matchday\"].browse(int(matchday_id)).exists()\n        if not matchday:\n            raise ValidationError(_(\"The match day no longer exists.\"))\n        self.env[\"federation.competition.role.assignment\"].assert_role(\n            matchday.edition_id, \"matchday_manager\", \"competition_director\"\n        )\n        return matchday\n\n    def _require_open(self, matchday):\n        if matchday.state != \"open\" or not matchday.active_session_id:\n            raise ValidationError(\n                _(\"Open the published match day before performing live operations.\")\n            )\n\n    @api.model\n    def open_matchday(self, matchday_id):\n        matchday = self._resolve(matchday_id)\n        publication = matchday.current_publication_id\n        if (\n            matchday.state != \"scheduled\"\n            or not publication\n            or publication.state != \"live\"\n        ):\n            raise ValidationError(\n                _(\"Only the current live published match day can be opened.\")\n            )\n        if publication.snapshot_digest != self.env[\n            \"federation.schedule.publication\"\n        ].digest_snapshot(publication.assignment_snapshot):\n            raise ValidationError(\n                _(\"The published schedule snapshot failed its integrity check.\")\n            )\n        session = (\n            self.env[\"federation.matchday.session\"]\n            .sudo()\n            .create(\n                {\n                    \"matchday_id\": matchday.id,\n                    \"publication_id\": publication.id,\n                    \"publication_digest\": publication.snapshot_digest,\n                }\n            )\n        )\n        for court in matchday.slot_ids.mapped(\"court_id\"):\n            self.env[\"federation.matchday.court.status\"].sudo().create(\n                {\"matchday_id\": matchday.id, \"court_id\": court.id}\n            )\n        matchday.sudo().state = \"open\"\n        self.env[\"federation.competition.event\"].emit(\n            matchday,\n            \"matchday_opened\",\n            {\"session_id\": session.id, \"publication_id\": publication.id},\n        )\n        return session\n\n    @api.model\n    def report_incident(self, matchday_id, incident_type, description):\n        matchday = self._resolve(matchday_id)\n        self._require_open(matchday)\n        return (\n            self.env[\"federation.matchday.incident\"]\n            .sudo()\n            .create(\n                {\n                    \"matchday_id\": matchday.id,\n                    \"incident_type\": incident_type,\n                    \"description\": description,\n                }\n            )\n        )\n\n    @api.model\n    def set_court_status(\n        self, matchday_id, court_id, state, delay_minutes=0, note=False\n    ):\n        matchday = self._resolve(matchday_id)\n        self._require_open(matchday)\n        status = self.env[\"federation.matchday.court.status\"].search(\n            [(\"matchday_id\", \"=\", matchday.id), (\"court_id\", \"=\", int(court_id))],\n            limit=1,\n        )\n        if not status:\n            raise ValidationError(_(\"The court is outside this match day.\"))\n        if state not in (\"available\", \"delayed\", \"unavailable\"):\n            raise ValidationError(_(\"Invalid court status.\"))\n        status.sudo().write(\n            {\n                \"state\": state,\n                \"delay_minutes\": max(0, int(delay_minutes or 0)),\n                \"note\": note,\n            }\n        )\n        if state != \"available\":\n            self.report_incident(\n                matchday.id,\n                \"court_unavailable\" if state == \"unavailable\" else \"delay\",\n                note or _(\"Court status changed.\"),\n            )\n        return status\n\n    @api.model\n    def resolve_incident(self, incident_id):\n        incident = (\n            self.env[\"federation.matchday.incident\"].browse(int(incident_id)).exists()\n        )\n        matchday = self._resolve(incident.matchday_id.id)\n        self._require_open(matchday)\n        incident.sudo().write(\n            {\n                \"resolved\": True,\n                \"resolved_at\": fields.Datetime.now(),\n                \"resolved_by_id\": self.env.user.id,\n            }\n        )\n        return True\n\n    @api.model\n    def record_schedule_deviation(\n        self,\n        matchday_id,\n        match_id,\n        deviation_type,\n        reason,\n        new_slot_id=False,\n        delay_minutes=0,\n    ):\n        matchday = self._resolve(matchday_id)\n        self._require_open(matchday)\n        if not (reason or \"\").strip():\n            raise ValidationError(_(\"Explain the operational schedule change.\"))\n        if deviation_type not in (\"move\", \"delay\", \"postpone\", \"cancel\"):\n            raise ValidationError(_(\"Invalid operational deviation type.\"))\n        match = self.env[\"federation.match\"].browse(int(match_id)).exists()\n        if (\n            not match\n            or match.schedule_publication_id != matchday.current_publication_id\n        ):\n            raise ValidationError(\n                _(\"The match is not part of the publication opened for this match day.\")\n            )\n        old_slot = match.operational_slot_id or match.published_slot_id\n        new_slot = self.env[\"federation.schedule.slot\"]\n        values = {}\n        if deviation_type == \"move\":\n            new_slot = (\n                self.env[\"federation.schedule.slot\"]\n                .browse(int(new_slot_id or 0))\n                .exists()\n            )\n            if (\n                not new_slot\n                or new_slot.matchday_id != matchday\n                or new_slot.state != \"available\"\n            ):\n                raise ValidationError(_(\"Select an available slot on this match day.\"))\n            occupied = self.env[\"federation.match\"].search(\n                [\n                    (\"operational_slot_id\", \"=\", new_slot.id),\n                    (\"id\", \"!=\", match.id),\n                    (\"operational_status\", \"not in\", (\"postponed\", \"cancelled\")),\n                ],\n                limit=1,\n            )\n            if occupied:\n                raise ValidationError(\n                    _(\"The selected operational slot is already occupied.\")\n                )\n            values = {\n                \"operational_slot_id\": new_slot.id,\n                \"operational_status\": \"moved\",\n                \"date_scheduled\": new_slot.start_datetime,\n            }\n        elif deviation_type == \"delay\":\n            minutes = int(delay_minutes or 0)\n            if minutes <= 0:\n                raise ValidationError(\n                    _(\"The delay must be a positive number of minutes.\")\n                )\n            base = match.date_scheduled or old_slot.start_datetime\n            values = {\n                \"operational_slot_id\": old_slot.id,\n                \"operational_status\": \"delayed\",\n                \"date_scheduled\": base + timedelta(minutes=minutes),\n            }\n        elif deviation_type == \"postpone\":\n            values = {\n                \"operational_slot_id\": False,\n                \"operational_status\": \"postponed\",\n                \"date_scheduled\": False,\n            }\n        else:\n            values = {\n                \"operational_slot_id\": False,\n  {"content": "\"\"\"Tour T-09: Public Site — Tournament Publication and Editorial Scheduling\n\nWalks the public site publication workflow:\n  1. Create a tournament and publish it (website_published = True)\n  2. Create an editorial item in draft\n  3. Schedule the item → 'scheduled'\n  4. Publish the item → 'published'\n  5. Archive the item → 'archived'\n  6. Reset to draft → 'draft'\n  7. Verify get_live_items() returns only published/in-window items\n\nKey invariants verified:\n- editorial item states: draft → scheduled → published → archived → draft\n- get_live_items() excludes draft and archived items\n- tournament website_published field is settable\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom odoo.tests.common import TransactionCase\n\n\nclass TestTourPublication(TransactionCase):\n    \"\"\"T-09: Public site publication lifecycle tour.\"\"\"\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls):\n        super().setUpClass()\n\n        cls.season = cls.env[\"federation.season\"].create(\n            {\n                \"name\": \"Publication Tour Season\",\n                \"code\": \"PUB26\",\n                \"date_start\": \"2026-01-01\",\n                \"date_end\": \"2026-12-31\",\n            }\n        )\n        cls.tournament = cls.env[\"federation.tournament\"].create(\n            {\n                \"name\": \"Publication Tour Cup\",\n                \"season_id\": cls.season.id,\n                \"date_start\": \"2026-06-01\",\n            }\n        )\n\n    def test_publication_lifecycle(self):\n        \"\"\"Tournament publication + editorial item state machine.\"\"\"\n\n        # STEP 1: Publish tournament\n        self.assertFalse(self.tournament.website_published)\n        self.tournament.website_published = True\n        self.assertTrue(self.tournament.website_published)\n\n        # STEP 2: Create an editorial item in draft\n        item = self.env[\"federation.public.editorial.item\"].create(\n            {\n                \"name\": \"Tour Launch Announcement\",\n                \"summary\": \"The Tour Cup 2026 is now open!\",\n                \"content_type\": \"announcement\",\n                \"publish_start\": \"2026-01-01 09:00:00\",\n                \"season_id\": self.season.id,\n                \"tournament_id\": self.tournament.id,\n            }\n        )\n        self.assertEqual(item.publication_state, \"draft\")\n        self.assertFalse(item.can_access_publicly())\n\n        # STEP 3: Schedule the item\n        item.action_schedule()\n        self.assertEqual(item.publication_state, \"scheduled\")\n\n        # STEP 4: Publish\n        item.action_publish()\n        self.assertEqual(item.publication_state, \"published\")\n        self.assertTrue(item.can_access_publicly())\n\n        # STEP 5: get_live_items returns published item\n        live = self.env[\"federation.public.editorial.item\"].get_live_items(\n            season=self.season\n        )\n        self.assertIn(item, live)\n\n        # STEP 6: Archive — removed from live feed\n        item.action_archive_item()\n        self.assertEqual(item.publication_state, \"archived\")\n        self.assertFalse(item.can_access_publicly())\n\n        live_after_archive = self.env[\n            \"federation.public.editorial.item\"\n        ].get_live_items(season=self.season)\n        self.assertNotIn(item, live_after_archive)\n\n        #  {"content": "# Schedule Approval\n\nThis addon owns independent schedule review and immutable schedule publication.\nIt does not own working assignments, calendar capacity, or match-day execution.\n\n## Operator flow\n\n1. A schedule planner submits a complete schedule from **Schedule Planner**.\n2. The addon creates immutable review evidence and opens the pending review.\n3. A different assigned schedule approver opens **Schedule Review Queue**.\n4. The approver either enters a note and selects **Request Changes**, or selects\n   **Approve Schedule**.\n5. Approved reviews appear under **Publication > Approved Schedules**.\n6. **Publish Schedule** creates any missing operational matches for the assigned\n   fixtures through the fixture materializer, creates an immutable publication,\n   links matches to their exact slots, and makes the publication current for the\n   match day.\n7. **Publication > Schedule Publications** retains live and superseded evidence.\n\nThe command service enforces edition roles, separation of duties, submitted\nrevision equality, snapshot-digest equality for schedule facts, and schedule\nrevalidation. Operational match links are derived and may be materialized at\npublication. Buttons are only orchestration entry points and never duplicate\nthe business rules.\nEach review records the authenticated schedule planner who submits it, so that\nplanner can withdraw a pending review before an approver decides it.\nRole assignments remain administrator-managed; the Schedule Approver group\nexposes the workflow actions, while a per-edition `schedule_approver` assignment\nauthorizes the operation. The role check performs a read-only internal lookup,\nand approval revalidation performs controlled read-only access to the submitted\ncalendar and venue data, so approvers do not need CRUD access to those\nadministrator-managed records. Reviewers may edit the note while a review is\npending; state and audit fields remain command-service-only.\n\n## Tests and contracts\n\n```bash\npython ci/check_competition_pipeline_contract.py\npython ci/check_schedule_handoff_contract.py\nbash ci/run_tests.sh --module sports_federation_schedule_approval\nbash ci/run_tests.sh --suite competition_core\n```\n\n## 5.1.1 integrity rules\n\nReview evidence is immutable through the model guard. Pending reviewers may edit\nonly the note; only the approval command service can write decision fields\nthrough a narrowly scoped context. Publications are live,\nversioned and replaced per match day, not per edition. Publication confirmation\nlocks the match day and verifies the expected current publication before\nallocating the next version. Replacements require a dedicated reason that is\nseparate from the review note.\n\n\n## Expanded match-day publication guard\n\nImmediately before replacing a live publication, the approval service validates the approved schedule against every fixture currently allocated to the physical match day. A division or fixture added after review invalidates that review for publication purposes. The planner must refresh the schedule, assign the newly exposed fixtures, and submit the complete replacement for review again. This guarantees that Match-Day Control and public publishing consume one complete multi-division publication.\n", "content_length": 3231, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "sports_federation_schedule_approval/README.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "faf496085aa52559da986e3f70bf9e58588aea7c2c2e5b761a31300c46b0aa88", "size_bytes": 3231}

 {"content": "# Sports Federation Scheduling\n\nThe scheduling addon owns working schedules, fixture assignments, schedule\nchanges, and the weighted fairness auto-schedule preview.\n\nThe auto-schedule wizard previews deterministic assignments after applying hard\nconstraints and weighted fairness penalties. Applying a proposal records the\nautomatic assignments and schedule changes while checking the schedule revision\nfor concurrent edits.\n\nAll access-control rows, including the auto-schedule wizard permissions, are\nloaded through `security/ir.model.access.csv`.\n\n\n## Operator handoff to independent review\n\nA planner completes fixture-to-slot assignments in **Schedule Planner** and uses\n**Submit for Review**. Blocking validation errors prevent submission. When only\nwarnings remain, a confirmation dialog requires an explicit justification. The\nbutton delegates to `federation.schedule.commands`; when Schedule Approval is\ninstalled, the approval extension creates the immutable pending review in the\nsame transaction and opens it directly.\n\nSubmitted schedules are immutable. If a reviewer requests changes, the schedule\nreturns to `changes_requested`, where the planner can edit and submit a new\nrevision. Planners can use the **Reviews** smart button to inspect the current\nhandoff and retained history.\n\n\n## Reversible planning\n\nDraft and change-requested schedules refresh the current calendar fixture pool. Late fixtures appear as unassigned work; stale assignments are flagged and never silently deleted. Pending submissions can be withdrawn, while published corrections use governed replacement revisions.\n\n\n## Multi-division match days\n\nA working schedule covers the complete physical match day, not only the division structure that originally opened the planner. If another division is allocated to the same match day during a governed revision, **Refresh Calendar Fixtures** exposes those fixtures as unassigned work and auto-scheduling includes them in its proposal. Submission remains blocked until every non-bye fixture from every match-day allocation is assigned.\n\nPublication validates the current match-day fixture pool again. If allocations change after review, publication stops and requires a refreshed, complete schedule and a new review instead of silently publishing an incomplete game day.\n\n## Club-aware automatic scheduling\n\nAutomatic scheduling treats simultaneous matches involving different teams of\nthe same club as the first fairness priority. It detects any overlapping slot\nwindows, not only slots with identical boundaries. After deterministic greedy\nconstruction, a bounded pairwise-swap improvement pass repairs avoidable club\nclashes while preserving all hard constraints and pinned manual or operational\nassignments. Remaining club clashes therefore indicate constrained capacity or\npinned assignments rather than an avoidable greedy choice.\n\n", "content_length": 2871, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "sports_federation_scheduling/README.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "01a61a812eeb85aef16c2a4468df9e02429b7f4c71152a09e06f997ff9a92a93", "size_bytes": 2871}

 # Workflow: Match Day Operations
@@ -5985,6 +5985,19 @@ Primary surfaces by phase:
 Direct queues and record pages exist as shortcuts, but they should not be
 treated as equal-weight starting points for club representatives.
 
+Draft match days may be deleted during preparation. Any draft or
+change-requested working schedules are removed with the match day so the
+restrictive schedule foreign key cannot leave an unusable planning record.
+Match days with review or publication history, or with live operations, remain
+protected and must use schedule revision or operational correction workflows.
+
+For test cleanup or an explicitly authorized destructive correction, a closed
+match day exposes **Delete Match Day** to federation and match-day managers. The
+wizard reports the affected schedule, publication, session, deviation, and
+match counts before requiring a reason and showing a final confirmation popup.
+It removes the match-day execution and publication evidence, while retaining
+competition match records detached from the deleted publication and slots.
+

+    def _delete_restartable_schedules(self):
+        """Remove every mutable schedule, including records hidden by rules."""
+        self.ensure_one()
+        self._assert_restartable_from_scratch()
+        schedules = (
+            self.env["federation.schedule"]
+            .sudo()
+            .with_context(active_test=False)
+            .search([("matchday_id", "=", self.id)])
+        )
+        schedules.unlink()
+
+    def _closed_delete_impact(self):
+        self.ensure_one()
+        Schedule = (
+            self.env["federation.schedule"].sudo().with_context(active_test=False)
+        )
+        schedules = Schedule.search([("matchday_id", "=", self.id)])
+        publications = (
+            self.env["federation.schedule.publication"]
+            .sudo()
+            .search([("schedule_id", "in", schedules.ids)])
+        )
+        sessions = (
+            self.env["federation.matchday.session"]
+            .sudo()
+            .search([("matchday_id", "=", self.id)])
+        )
+        deviations = (
+            self.env["federation.matchday.deviation"]
+            .sudo()
+            .search([("matchday_id", "=", self.id)])
+        )
+        slots = (
+            self.env["federation.schedule.slot"]
+            .sudo()
+            .search([("matchday_id", "=", self.id)])
+        )
+        matches = (
+            self.env["federation.match"]
+            .sudo()
+            .search(
+                [
+                    "|",
+                    "|",
+                    ("schedule_publication_id", "in", publications.ids),
+                    ("published_slot_id", "in", slots.ids),
+                    ("operational_slot_id", "in", slots.ids),
+                ]
+            )
+        )
+        return {
+            "schedule_count": len(schedules),
+            "publication_count": len(publications),
+            "session_count": len(sessions),
+            "deviation_count": len(deviations),
+            "match_count": len(matches),
+        }
+
+    def _delete_closed_matchday_data(self):
+        """Remove closed-day data only after explicit destructive confirmation."""
+        self.ensure_one()
+        if self.state != "closed":
+            raise ValidationError(_("Only closed match days can use this action."))
+        if (
+            self.env.context.get("matchday_destructive_delete_token")
+            is not MATCHDAY_DESTRUCTIVE_DELETE_TOKEN
+        ):
+            raise ValidationError(
+                _(
+                    "Closed match days contain execution and publication history. "
+                    "Use Delete Match Day and confirm the impact before removing it."
+                )
+            )
+
+        Schedule = (
+            self.env["federation.schedule"].sudo().with_context(active_test=False)
+        )
+        schedules = Schedule.search([("matchday_id", "=", self.id)])
+        reviews = (
+            self.env["federation.schedule.review"]
+            .sudo()
+            .search([("schedule_id", "in", schedules.ids)])
+        )
+        publications = (
+            self.env["federation.schedule.publication"]
+            .sudo()
+            .search([("schedule_id", "in", schedules.ids)])
+        )
+        slots = (
+            self.env["federation.schedule.slot"]
+            .sudo()
+            .search([("matchday_id", "=", self.id)])
+        )
+        matches = (
+            self.env["federation.match"]
+            .sudo()
+            .search(
+                [
+                    "|",
+                    "|",
+                    ("schedule_publication_id", "in", publications.ids),
+                    ("published_slot_id", "in", slots.ids),
+                    ("operational_slot_id", "in", slots.ids),
+                ]
+            )
+        )
+
+        self.sudo().write({"current_publication_id": False})
+        matches.write(
+            {
+                "schedule_publication_id": False,
+                "published_slot_id": False,
+                "operational_slot_id": False,
+            }
+        )
+        self.env["federation.matchday.deviation"].sudo().with_context(
+            matchday_destructive_delete_token=MATCHDAY_DESTRUCTIVE_DELETE_TOKEN
+        ).search([("matchday_id", "=", self.id)]).unlink()
+        self.env["federation.matchday.session"].sudo().with_context(
+            matchday_destructive_delete_token=MATCHDAY_DESTRUCTIVE_DELETE_TOKEN
+        ).search([("matchday_id", "=", self.id)]).unlink()
+        reviews.with_context(
+            matchday_destructive_delete_token=MATCHDAY_DESTRUCTIVE_DELETE_TOKEN
+        ).unlink()
+        publications.with_context(
+            matchday_destructive_delete_token=MATCHDAY_DESTRUCTIVE_DELETE_TOKEN
+        ).unlink()
+
+        related_schedules = Schedule.search(
+            [
+                "|",
+                ("supersedes_id", "in", schedules.ids),
+                ("superseded_by_id", "in", schedules.ids),
+            ]
+        )
+        (schedules | related_schedules).write(
+            {"supersedes_id": False, "superseded_by_id": False}
+        )
+        schedules.with_context(
+            matchday_destructive_delete_token=MATCHDAY_DESTRUCTIVE_DELETE_TOKEN
+        ).unlink()
+
+    def unlink(self):
+        """Delete draft match days together with their mutable schedules.
+
+        ``federation.schedule.matchday_id`` deliberately uses a restrictive
+        foreign key so published schedule history cannot be orphaned. Draft
+        schedules are disposable planning data, however, and should not make
+        an otherwise deletable draft match day impossible to remove.
+        """
+        for matchday in self:
+            if matchday.state == "closed":
+                matchday._delete_closed_matchday_data()
+            else:
+                matchday._delete_restartable_schedules()
+        return super().unlink()
+
+    def action_open_closed_delete(self):
+        self.ensure_one()
+        self.env["federation.competition.role.assignment"].assert_role(
+            self.edition_id, "matchday_manager", "competition_director"
+        )
+        if self.state != "closed":
+            raise ValidationError(_("Only closed match days can be deleted here."))
+        wizard = self.env["federation.matchday.delete.wizard"].create(
+            {"matchday_id": self.id}
+        )
+        return {
+            "type": "ir.actions.act_window",
+            "name": _("Delete Closed Match Day"),
+            "res_model": "federation.matchday.delete.wizard",
+            "res_id": wizard.id,
+            "view_mode": "form",
+            "target": "new",
+        }
+

+from odoo import _, api, fields, models
+from odoo.exceptions import ValidationError
+
+from odoo.addons.sports_federation_base.destructive_tokens import (
+    MATCHDAY_DESTRUCTIVE_DELETE_TOKEN,
+)
+
+
+class FederationMatchdayDeleteWizard(models.TransientModel):
+    _name = "federation.matchday.delete.wizard"
+    _description = "Delete Closed Match Day"
+
+    matchday_id = fields.Many2one(
+        "federation.matchday", required=True, readonly=True, ondelete="cascade"
+    )
+    impact_summary = fields.Text(compute="_compute_impact_summary")
+    reason = fields.Text(string="Reason")
+
+    @api.depends("matchday_id")
+    def _compute_impact_summary(self):
+        for wizard in self:
+            if not wizard.matchday_id:
+                wizard.impact_summary = False
+                continue
+            impact = wizard.matchday_id._closed_delete_impact()
+            wizard.impact_summary = _(
+                "This will permanently delete the match day and its working "
+                "schedule data: %(schedules)s schedule(s), %(publications)s "
+                "publication(s), %(sessions)s execution session(s), and "
+                "%(deviations)s operational deviation(s). %(matches)s match(es) "
+                "will be detached from the deleted publication and calendar "
+                "slots, but the match records will remain in the competition.",
+                schedules=impact["schedule_count"],
+                publications=impact["publication_count"],
+                sessions=impact["session_count"],
+                deviations=impact["deviation_count"],
+                matches=impact["match_count"],
+            )
+
+    def action_delete(self):
+        self.ensure_one()
+        reason = (self.reason or "").strip()
+        if not reason:
+            raise ValidationError(
+                _("Enter a reason before permanently deleting the match day.")
+            )
+        matchday = self.matchday_id.exists()
+        if not matchday:
+            raise ValidationError(_("The match day no longer exists."))
+        if matchday.state != "closed":
+            raise ValidationError(
+                _("This action is only available for closed match days.")
+            )
+        self.env["federation.competition.role.assignment"].assert_role(
+            matchday.edition_id, "matchday_manager", "competition_director"
+        )
+        matchday.with_context(
+            matchday_destructive_delete_token=MATCHDAY_DESTRUCTIVE_DELETE_TOKEN,
+            matchday_delete_reason=reason,
+        ).unlink()
+        return {
+            "type": "ir.actions.act_window_close",
+        }
+

 working schedules first. Published, reviewed, open, and closed match days remain
 protected and must use the normal revision or operational correction workflows.
 
+## Deleting closed test data
+
+Federation managers and match-day managers can use **Delete Match Day** on a
+closed match day. The confirmation wizard shows the number of schedules,
+publications, execution sessions, deviations, and matches affected. Confirming
+the deletion permanently removes the match-day planning and execution evidence;
+linked match records remain in the competition but are detached from the deleted
+publication and calendar slots. The action requires a reason and displays a
+final confirmation popup before deletion.
+

@@ -65,6 +69,137 @@ class FederationMatchdayRestart(models.Model):
         )
         schedules.unlink()
 
+    def _closed_delete_impact(self):
+        self.ensure_one()
+        Schedule = (
+            self.env["federation.schedule"].sudo().with_context(active_test=False)
+        )
+        schedules = Schedule.search([("matchday_id", "=", self.id)])
+        publications = (
+            self.env["federation.schedule.publication"]
+            .sudo()
+            .search([("schedule_id", "in", schedules.ids)])
+        )
+        sessions = (
+            self.env["federation.matchday.session"]
+            .sudo()
+            .search([("matchday_id", "=", self.id)])
+        )
+        deviations = (
+            self.env["federation.matchday.deviation"]
+            .sudo()
+            .search([("matchday_id", "=", self.id)])
+        )
+        slots = (
+            self.env["federation.schedule.slot"]
+            .sudo()
+            .search([("matchday_id", "=", self.id)])
+        )
+        matches = (
+            self.env["federation.match"]
+            .sudo()
+            .search(
+                [
+                    "|",
+                    "|",
+                    ("schedule_publication_id", "in", publications.ids),
+                    ("published_slot_id", "in", slots.ids),
+                    ("operational_slot_id", "in", slots.ids),
+                ]
+            )
+        )
+        return {
+            "schedule_count": len(schedules),
+            "publication_count": len(publications),
+            "session_count": len(sessions),
+            "deviation_count": len(deviations),
+            "match_count": len(matches),
+        }
+
+    def _delete_closed_matchday_data(self):
+        """Remove closed-day data only after explicit destructive confirmation."""
+        self.ensure_one()
+        if self.state != "closed":
+            raise ValidationError(_("Only closed match days can use this action."))
+        if (
+            self.env.context.get("matchday_destructive_delete_token")
+            is not MATCHDAY_DESTRUCTIVE_DELETE_TOKEN
+        ):
+            raise ValidationError(
+                _(
+                    "Closed match days contain execution and publication history. "
+                    "Use Delete Match Day and confirm the impact before removing it."
+                )
+            )
+
+        Schedule = (
+            self.env["federation.schedule"].sudo().with_context(active_test=False)
+        )
